*Jiacheng Wang,Jinchang Hou,Fabian Wang,Ping Jian,Chenfu Bao,Zhonghou Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出内在风险审计概念，建立HINTBench基准测试集，揭示现有LLM在智能体内在风险检测、定位和诊断方面的能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体安全评估主要关注外部诱导风险，但智能体在良性条件下仍可能进入不安全轨迹。论文研究这种被忽视的内在风险设置，其中内在故障保持潜伏状态，在长期执行中传播，最终导致严重后果。

Method: 引入非攻击性内在风险审计方法，构建HINTBench基准测试集，包含629个智能体轨迹（523个风险轨迹，106个安全轨迹，平均33步），支持三个任务：风险检测、风险步骤定位和内在故障类型识别。注释按照统一的五约束分类法组织。

Result: 实验显示显著能力差距：强LLM在轨迹级风险检测上表现良好，但在风险步骤定位上的性能降至35 Strict-F1以下，细粒度故障诊断更加困难。现有防护模型在此设置下迁移效果差。

Conclusion: 内在风险审计是智能体安全领域的一个开放挑战，需要开发新的方法来检测和定位智能体执行过程中的内在风险。

Abstract: Existing agent-safety evaluation has focused mainly on externally induced risks. Yet agents may still enter unsafe trajectories under benign conditions. We study this complementary but underexplored setting through the lens of \emph{intrinsic} risk, where intrinsic failures remain latent, propagate across long-horizon execution, and eventually lead to high-consequence outcomes. To evaluate this setting, we introduce \emph{non-attack intrinsic risk auditing} and present \textbf{HINTBench}, a benchmark of 629 agent trajectories (523 risky, 106 safe; 33 steps on average) supporting three tasks: risk detection, risk-step localization, and intrinsic failure-type identification. Its annotations are organized under a unified five-constraint taxonomy. Experiments reveal a substantial capability gap: strong LLMs perform well on trajectory-level risk detection, but their performance drops to below 35 Strict-F1 on risk-step localization, while fine-grained failure diagnosis proves even harder. Existing guard models transfer poorly to this setting. These findings establish intrinsic risk auditing as an open challenge for agent safety.

</details>


### [120] [Unsupervised Anomaly Detection in Process-Complex Industrial Time Series: A Real-World Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13928)
*Sergej Krasnikov,Lukas Meitz,Samineh Bagheri,Michael Heider,Thorsten Schöler,Jörg Hähner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了不同异常检测方法在复杂工业时序数据上的性能，发现传统方法（如孤立森林）无法有效处理非周期性多尺度动态，而自编码器表现更好，其中时序卷积自编码器最为稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业时序数据比常用基准数据集复杂得多，主要由于异构、多阶段的操作过程。在简化条件下验证的异常检测方法往往无法泛化到工业环境，因此需要评估哪些模型能够处理这种复杂性。

Method: 使用从完全运行的工业机械收集的独特数据集进行实证研究，评估多种模型类别：从经典的孤立森林基线扩展到多种自编码器架构（包括时序卷积自编码器、循环自编码器和变分自编码器）。

Result: 孤立森林无法有效建模数据中的非周期性多尺度动态，而自编码器表现更好。其中时序卷积自编码器实现最稳健的性能，循环和变分自编码器需要更仔细的调参。

Conclusion: 工业时序数据的复杂性需要专门的异常检测方法，自编码器（特别是时序卷积架构）比传统方法更适合处理工业环境中的异构、多阶段过程动态。

Abstract: Industrial time-series data from real production environments exhibits substantially higher complexity than commonly used benchmark datasets, primarily due to heterogeneous, multi-stage operational processes. As a result, anomaly detection methods validated under simplified conditions often fail to generalize to industrial settings. This work presents an empirical study on a unique dataset collected from fully operational industrial machinery, explicitly capturing pronounced process-induced variability.
  We evaluate which model classes are capable of capturing this complexity, starting with a classical Isolation Forest baseline and extending to multiple autoencoder architectures. Experimental results show that Isolation Forest is insufficient for modeling the non-periodic, multi-scale dynamics present in the data, whereas autoencoders consistently perform better. Among them, temporal convolutional autoencoders achieve the most robust performance, while recurrent and variational variants require more careful tuning.

</details>


### [121] [From $P(y|x)$ to $P(y)$: Investigating Reinforcement Learning in Pre-train Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14142)
*Yuqiao Tan,Minzheng Wang,Bo Liu,Zichen Liu,Tian Liang,Shizhu He,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出PreRL方法，直接在预训练空间P(y)上进行奖励驱动的在线更新，通过负样本强化(NSR)机制有效驱动推理能力，并最终提出双空间强化学习(DSRL)策略，在预训练空间进行推理空间剪枝后再进行标准RL微调优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)虽然能优化条件分布P(y|x)，但其潜力受限于基础模型的现有输出分布。优化预训练空间的边际分布P(y)能解决这一瓶颈，但传统预训练依赖静态语料库的被动学习，导致分布偏移，阻碍了有针对性的推理能力提升。

Method: 提出PreRL方法，直接在预训练空间的边际分布P(y)上进行奖励驱动的在线更新；发现负样本强化(NSR)机制在PreRL中作为推理的有效驱动力；最终提出双空间强化学习(DSRL)策略，采用策略重生方法，先用NSR-PreRL初始化模型以扩展推理视野，然后过渡到标准RL进行细粒度优化。

Result: 理论和实证验证了log P(y)和log P(y|x)之间的强梯度对齐，证明PreRL可作为标准RL的可行替代；NSR-PreRL能快速剪枝错误推理空间，同时刺激内源性反思行为，使转换思维和反思思维分别增加14.89倍和6.54倍；DSRL在广泛实验中始终优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 预训练空间剪枝能有效引导策略进入精炼的正确推理子空间，双空间强化学习(DSRL)策略通过预训练空间推理能力扩展和标准RL细粒度优化的结合，实现了比传统方法更优的推理性能提升。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) significantly enhances LLM reasoning by optimizing the conditional distribution P(y|x), its potential is fundamentally bounded by the base model's existing output distribution. Optimizing the marginal distribution P(y) in the Pre-train Space addresses this bottleneck by encoding reasoning ability and preserving broad exploration capacity. Yet, conventional pre-training relies on static corpora for passive learning, leading to a distribution shift that hinders targeted reasoning enhancement. In this paper, we introduce PreRL (Pre-train Space RL), which applies reward-driven online updates directly to P(y). We theoretically and empirically validate the strong gradient alignment between log P(y) and log P(y|x), establishing PreRL as a viable surrogate for standard RL. Furthermore, we uncover a critical mechanism: Negative Sample Reinforcement (NSR) within PreRL serves as an exceptionally effective driver for reasoning. NSR-PreRL rapidly prunes incorrect reasoning spaces while stimulating endogenous reflective behaviors, increasing transition and reflection thoughts by 14.89x and 6.54x, respectively. Leveraging these insights, we propose Dual Space RL (DSRL), a Policy Reincarnation strategy that initializes models with NSR-PreRL to expand the reasoning horizon before transitioning to standard RL for fine-grained optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DSRL consistently outperforms strong baselines, proving that pre-train space pruning effectively steers the policy toward a refined correct reasoning subspace.

</details>


### [122] [PRiMeFlow: Capturing Complex Expression Heterogeneity in Perturbation Response Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13986)
*Zichao Yan,Yan Wu,Mica Xu Ji,Chaitra Agrahar,Esther Wershof,Marcel Nassar,Mehrshad Sadria,Ridvan Eksi,Vladimir Trifonov,Ignacio Ibarra,Telmo Felgueira,Błażej Osiński,Rory Stark*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRiMeFlow是一种基于流匹配的端到端方法，直接在基因表达空间中建模遗传和小分子扰动效应，能准确近似单细胞基因表达的经验分布，在PerturBench基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测细胞状态扰动的计算效应可以大规模识别细胞行为驱动因素并加速药物发现，但单细胞基因表达的异质性和复杂的潜在基因依赖关系给建模带来挑战。

Method: 提出PRiMeFlow，一种基于流匹配的端到端方法，直接在基因表达空间中建模遗传和小分子扰动效应，使用U-Net架构参数化速度场。

Result: 在PerturBench中进行广泛基准测试，证明PRiMeFlow能准确近似单细胞基因表达的经验分布；通过消融研究验证了在基因表达空间操作和使用U-Net架构等重要设计选择；基于PRiMeFlow架构的模型在首届ARC虚拟细胞挑战赛中获得了通用奖。

Conclusion: PRiMeFlow通过分布拟合方法有效解决了单细胞基因表达建模的挑战，为大规模预测细胞扰动效应提供了有效工具，在基准测试和实际挑战中验证了其优越性能。

Abstract: Predicting the effects of perturbations in-silico on cell state can identify drivers of cell behavior at scale and accelerate drug discovery. However, modeling challenges remain due to the inherent heterogeneity of single cell gene expression and the complex, latent gene dependencies. Here, we present PRiMeFlow, an end-to-end flow matching based approach to directly model the effects of genetic and small molecule perturbations in the gene expression space. The distribution-fitting approach taken by PRiMeFlow enables it to accurately approximate the empirical distribution of single-cell gene expression, which we demonstrate through extensive benchmarking inside PerturBench. Through ablation studies, we also validate important model design choices such as operating in gene expression space and parameterizing the velocity field with a U-Net architecture. The PRiMeFlow architecture was used as the basis for the model that won the Generalist Prize in the first ARC Virtual Cell Challenge.

</details>


### [123] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Methane Sorption: Cross-Gas Transfer Learning, Ensemble Collapse Under Physics Constraints, and Monte Carlo Dropout Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13992)
*Mohammad Nooraiepour,Zezhang Song,Wei Li,Sarah Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息迁移学习框架，将氢吸附PINN通过弹性权重巩固、煤特定特征工程和三阶段课程学习迁移到甲烷吸附预测，在993个煤样数据上实现R²=0.932，比传统等温线提升227%，同时采用五种贝叶斯不确定性量化方法比较性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有甲烷吸附预测模型缺乏结合热力学一致性、跨数据稀缺地质系统的有效知识迁移以及校准不确定性估计的能力，需要开发能够同时解决这些问题的统一框架。

Method: 采用物理信息迁移学习框架：1) 通过弹性权重巩固将氢吸附PINN迁移到甲烷吸附预测；2) 煤特定特征工程；3) 三阶段课程学习逐步平衡迁移保留与热力学微调；4) 使用五种贝叶斯不确定性量化方法（蒙特卡洛Dropout、深度集成等）评估不确定性。

Result: 在114个独立煤实验的993个平衡测量数据上，框架在保留煤样上达到R²=0.932，比仅基于压力的经典等温线提升227%；氢预训练比随机初始化降低18.9%RMSE并加速19.4%收敛；蒙特卡洛Dropout在最小开销下实现良好校准的不确定性，而深度集成因共享物理约束而性能下降。

Conclusion: 蒙特卡洛Dropout是该物理约束迁移学习框架中性能最佳的不确定性量化方法，跨气体迁移学习是地质材料建模的数据高效策略，学习到的表征保持物理可解释性并与煤吸附机制一致。

Abstract: Accurate methane sorption prediction across heterogeneous coal ranks requires models that combine thermodynamic consistency, efficient knowledge transfer across data-scarce geological systems, and calibrated uncertainty estimates, capabilities that are rarely addressed together in existing frameworks. We present a physics-informed transfer learning framework that adapts a hydrogen sorption PINN to methane sorption prediction via Elastic Weight Consolidation, coal-specific feature engineering, and a three-phase curriculum that progressively balances transfer preservation with thermodynamic fine-tuning. Trained on 993 equilibrium measurements from 114 independent coal experiments spanning lignite to anthracite, the framework achieves R2 = 0.932 on held-out coal samples, a 227% improvement over pressure-only classical isotherms, while hydrogen pre-training delivers 18.9% lower RMSE and 19.4% faster convergence than random initialization. Five Bayesian uncertainty quantification approaches reveal a systematic divergence in performance across physics-constrained architectures. Monte Carlo Dropout achieves well-calibrated uncertainty at minimal overhead, while deep ensembles, regardless of architectural diversity or initialization strategy, exhibit performance degradation because shared physics constraints narrow the admissible solution manifold. SHAP and ALE analyses confirm that learned representations remain physically interpretable and aligned with established coal sorption mechanisms: moisture-volatile interactions are most influential, pressure-temperature coupling captures thermodynamic co-dependence, and features exhibit non-monotonic effects. These results identify Monte Carlo Dropout as the best-performing UQ method in this physics-constrained transfer learning framework, and demonstrate cross-gas transfer learning as a data-efficient strategy for geological material modeling.

</details>


### [124] [LongCoT: Benchmarking Long-Horizon Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14140)
*Sumeet Ramesh Motwani,Daniel Nichols,Charles London,Peggy Li,Fabio Pizzati,Acer Blake,Hasan Hammoud,Tavish McDonald,Akshat Naik,Alesia Ivanova,Vignesh Baskaran,Ivan Laptev,Ruben Glatt,Tal Ben-Nun,Philip Torr,Natasha Jaques,Ameya Prabhu,Brian Bartoldson,Bhavya Kailkhura,Christian Schroeder de Witt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongCoT是一个包含2500个专家设计问题的基准测试，用于直接衡量前沿模型的长链思维推理能力，结果显示当前最佳模型准确率低于10%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型被部署用于复杂自主任务，其准确进行长时程推理的能力变得至关重要。当前需要一种能够直接测量长链思维推理能力的基准测试，以评估模型在需要大量推理步骤的复杂任务中的表现。

Method: 引入LongCoT基准测试，包含2500个专家设计的问题，涵盖化学、数学、计算机科学、国际象棋和逻辑等领域。每个问题由简短输入和可验证答案组成，解决需要导航包含数十到数十万推理token的相互依赖步骤图。每个局部步骤对前沿模型都是可处理的，因此失败反映了长时程推理的局限性。

Result: 发布时，最佳模型在LongCoT上的准确率低于10%（GPT 5.2：9.8%；Gemini 3 Pro：6.1%），揭示了当前能力的显著差距。

Conclusion: LongCoT为长时程推理提供了严格的衡量标准，能够跟踪前沿模型在长时间内可靠推理的能力，表明当前模型在长链思维推理方面仍有显著不足。

Abstract: As language models are increasingly deployed for complex autonomous tasks, their ability to reason accurately over longer horizons becomes critical. An essential component of this ability is planning and managing a long, complex chain-of-thought (CoT). We introduce LongCoT, a scalable benchmark of 2,500 expert-designed problems spanning chemistry, mathematics, computer science, chess, and logic to isolate and directly measure the long-horizon CoT reasoning capabilities of frontier models. Problems consist of a short input with a verifiable answer; solving them requires navigating a graph of interdependent steps that span tens to hundreds of thousands of reasoning tokens. Each local step is individually tractable for frontier models, so failures reflect long-horizon reasoning limitations. At release, the best models achieve <10% accuracy (GPT 5.2: 9.8%; Gemini 3 Pro: 6.1%) on LongCoT, revealing a substantial gap in current capabilities. Overall, LongCoT provides a rigorous measure of long-horizon reasoning, tracking the ability of frontier models to reason reliably over extended periods.

</details>


### [125] [Neural architectures for resolving references in program code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14073)
*Gergő Szalay,Gergely Zsolt Kovács,Sándor Teleki,Balázs Pintér,Tibor Gregorics*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对编程语言中的引用重写问题，提出了新的序列到序列架构，在直接和间接索引排列任务上显著优于基线模型，并将改进应用于实际反编译任务中


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受实际反编译任务启发，将引用重写抽象为直接和间接索引排列问题，现有序列到序列架构在这些任务上表现不佳

Method: 创建合成基准测试，引入新的序列到序列架构处理直接和间接索引排列问题，通过消融研究验证架构组件的重要性

Result: 新架构在鲁棒性和可扩展性上优于基线，能处理长度增加10倍的示例；在反编译switch语句的实际任务中，错误率降低42%

Conclusion: 提出的新架构有效解决了索引排列问题，显著提升了实际反编译任务的性能，所有架构组件都经过验证是必要的

Abstract: Resolving and rewriting references is fundamental in programming languages. Motivated by a real-world decompilation task, we abstract reference rewriting into the problems of direct and indirect indexing by permutation. We create synthetic benchmarks for these tasks and show that well-known sequence-to-sequence machine learning architectures are struggling on these benchmarks. We introduce new sequence-to-sequence architectures for both problems. Our measurements show that our architectures outperform the baselines in both robustness and scalability: our models can handle examples that are ten times longer compared to the best baseline. We measure the impact of our architecture in the real-world task of decompiling switch statements, which has an indexing subtask. According to our measurements, the extended model decreases the error rate by 42%. Multiple ablation studies show that all components of our architectures are essential.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Exploration and Exploitation Errors Are Measurable for Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13151)
*Jaden Park,Jungtaek Kim,Jongwon Jeong,Robert D. Nowak,Kangwook Lee,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个用于量化语言模型智能体探索与利用行为的方法框架，通过可控的2D网格环境和任务DAG设计，实现了无需访问内部策略的探索-利用评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型智能体在复杂开放式决策任务中需要有效平衡探索与利用，但缺乏无需访问内部策略就能系统区分和量化这两种行为的评估方法。

Method: 设计可控的2D网格环境和未知任务DAG，通过程序化调整地图生成来强调探索或利用难度；设计无需策略的评估指标，量化智能体行动中的探索与利用错误。

Result: 评估多种前沿语言模型智能体发现，即使最先进的模型在该任务上也表现不佳，不同模型展现出不同的失败模式；推理模型表现更好，且通过最小化工程改进可显著提升探索与利用能力。

Conclusion: 提出了一个可量化评估语言模型智能体探索与利用行为的框架，揭示了当前模型的局限性，并展示了通过工程改进提升性能的可能性，为未来研究提供了基准测试工具。

Abstract: Language Model (LM) agents are increasingly used in complex open-ended decision-making tasks, from AI coding to physical AI. A core requirement in these settings is the ability to both explore the problem space and exploit acquired knowledge effectively. However, systematically distinguishing and quantifying exploration and exploitation from observed actions without access to the agent's internal policy remains challenging. To address this, we design controllable environments inspired by practical embodied AI scenarios. Each environment consists of a partially observable 2D grid map and an unknown task Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG). The map generation can be programmatically adjusted to emphasize exploration or exploitation difficulty. To enable policy-agnostic evaluation, we design a metric to quantify exploration and exploitation errors from agent's actions. We evaluate a variety of frontier LM agents and find that even state-of-the-art models struggle on our task, with different models exhibiting distinct failure modes. We further observe that reasoning models solve the task more effectively and show both exploration and exploitation can be significantly improved through minimal harness engineering. We release our code \href{https://github.com/jjj-madison/measurable-explore-exploit}{here}.

</details>


### [127] [SciFi: A Safe, Lightweight, User-Friendly, and Fully Autonomous Agentic AI Workflow for Scientific Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13180)
*Qibin Liu,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个安全、轻量、用户友好的智能体框架，用于自主执行定义明确的科学任务，结合隔离执行环境、三层智能体循环和自评估do-until机制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体AI系统在现实世界科学研究中实现可靠部署仍面临重大挑战，需要解决安全性、可靠性和易用性问题

Method: 框架包含：1) 隔离执行环境确保安全；2) 三层智能体循环结构；3) 自评估do-until机制；4) 有效利用不同能力水平的大型语言模型；5) 专注于具有明确定义上下文和停止条件的结构化任务

Result: 实现了端到端自动化，最小化人工干预，支持研究人员将常规工作负载转移，专注于创造性活动和开放式科学探究

Conclusion: 该框架为科学研究提供了一个安全可靠的智能体解决方案，平衡了自主性与安全性，促进了人机协作的研究模式

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic AI have enabled increasingly autonomous workflows, but existing systems still face substantial challenges in achieving reliable deployment in real-world scientific research. In this work, we present a safe, lightweight, and user-friendly agentic framework for the autonomous execution of well-defined scientific tasks. The framework combines an isolated execution environment, a three-layer agent loop, and a self-assessing do-until mechanism to ensure safe and reliable operation while effectively leveraging large language models of varying capability levels. By focusing on structured tasks with clearly defined context and stopping criteria, the framework supports end-to-end automation with minimal human intervention, enabling researchers to offload routine workloads and devote more effort to creative activities and open-ended scientific inquiry.

</details>


### [128] [Numerical Instability and Chaos: Quantifying the Unpredictability of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13206)
*Chashi Mahiul Islam,Alan Villarreal,Mao Nishino,Shaeke Salman,Xiuwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文分析了大型语言模型在有限浮点精度下的数值不稳定性，揭示了早期层的"雪崩效应"和三种混沌行为机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地集成到智能体工作流中，其数值不稳定性导致的不可预测性已成为关键的可靠性问题。虽然已有研究展示了这些不稳定性对下游任务的显著影响，但其根本原因和底层机制仍不清楚。

Method: 采用严格的数值分析方法，追踪舍入误差在Transformer计算层中的传播、放大或消散过程。具体识别了早期层的"雪崩效应"，并系统分析了LLM在不同尺度下表现出的三种混沌行为机制。

Result: 发现LLM存在三种普遍且尺度依赖的混沌行为机制：1)稳定机制，扰动低于输入相关阈值而消失；2)混沌机制，舍入误差主导并驱动输出发散；3)信号主导机制，真实输入变化覆盖数值噪声。这些发现在多个数据集和模型架构中得到验证。

Conclusion: LLM的不可预测性根源于有限浮点精度，早期层的"雪崩效应"和三种混沌行为机制共同解释了模型输出的数值不稳定性，这对提高LLM在智能体工作流中的可靠性具有重要意义。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into agentic workflows, their unpredictability stemming from numerical instability has emerged as a critical reliability issue. While recent studies have demonstrated the significant downstream effects of these instabilities, the root causes and underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this paper, we present a rigorous analysis of how unpredictability is rooted in the finite numerical precision of floating-point representations, tracking how rounding errors propagate, amplify, or dissipate through Transformer computation layers. Specifically, we identify a chaotic "avalanche effect" in the early layers, where minor perturbations trigger binary outcomes: either rapid amplification or complete attenuation. Beyond specific error instances, we demonstrate that LLMs exhibit universal, scale-dependent chaotic behaviors characterized by three distinct regimes: 1) a stable regime, where perturbations fall below an input-dependent threshold and vanish, resulting in constant outputs; 2) a chaotic regime, where rounding errors dominate and drive output divergence; and 3) a signal-dominated regime, where true input variations override numerical noise. We validate these findings extensively across multiple datasets and model architectures.

</details>


### [129] [ReSS: Learning Reasoning Models for Tabular Data Prediction via Symbolic Scaffold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13392)
*Chenlang Yi,Gang Li,Zizhan Xiong,Tue Minh Cao,Yanmin Gong,My T. Thai,Tianbao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReSS框架结合符号与神经推理模型，通过决策树提取符号支架指导LLM生成忠实推理，增强表格数据预测的准确性和可解释性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决表格数据预测中高准确性与忠实、可理解推理的双重挑战。符号模型缺乏语义表达能力，而通用LLM需要专门微调才能掌握领域特定的表格推理

Method: 提出ReSS框架：1) 使用决策树模型提取实例级决策路径作为符号支架；2) 结合输入特征和标签，指导LLM生成严格遵循决策逻辑的自然语言推理；3) 利用生成的高质量数据集微调预训练LLM；4) 采用支架不变的数据增强策略提升泛化能力和可解释性

Result: 在医疗和金融基准测试中，ReSS训练模型相比传统决策树和标准微调方法提升高达10%，同时产生忠实且一致的推理

Conclusion: ReSS框架成功桥接了符号与神经推理模型，解决了表格数据预测中的准确性与可解释性平衡问题，通过定量评估指标验证了其生成推理的忠实性

Abstract: Tabular data remains prevalent in high-stakes domains such as healthcare and finance, where predictive models are expected to provide both high accuracy and faithful, human-understandable reasoning. While symbolic models offer verifiable logic, they lack semantic expressiveness. Meanwhile, general-purpose LLMs often require specialized fine-tuning to master domain-specific tabular reasoning. To address the dual challenges of scalable data curation and reasoning consistency, we propose ReSS, a systematic framework that bridges symbolic and neural reasoning models. ReSS leverages a decision-tree model to extract instance-level decision paths as symbolic scaffolds. These scaffolds, alongside input features and labels, guide an LLM to generate grounded natural-language reasoning that strictly adheres to the underlying decision logic. The resulting high-quality dataset is used to fine-tune a pretrained LLM into a specialized tabular reasoning model, further enhanced by a scaffold-invariant data augmentation strategy to improve generalization and explainability. To rigorously assess faithfulness, we introduce quantitative metrics including hallucination rate, explanation necessity, and explanation sufficiency. Experimental results on medical and financial benchmarks demonstrate that ReSS-trained models improve traditional decision trees and standard fine-tuning approaches up to $10\%$ while producing faithful and consistent reasoning

</details>


### [130] [Quantifying and Understanding Uncertainty in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13395)
*Yangyi Li,Chenxu Zhao,Mengdi Huai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种为大型推理模型提供统计保证的推理-答案不确定性量化方法，以及基于Shapley值的可解释性框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统不确定性量化方法无法为推理-答案生成提供有限样本保证，且忽视推理轨迹与最终答案的逻辑联系，缺乏对不确定性来源的可解释性分析

Method: 1) 提出量化推理-答案结构不确定性的新方法，具有统计保证；2) 开发基于Shapley值的统一示例-步骤解释框架，识别保持保证所需的训练示例子集和关键推理步骤

Result: 在具有挑战性的推理数据集上进行广泛实验，验证了所提方法的有效性

Conclusion: 该方法为大型推理模型提供了具有统计保证的不确定性量化，同时实现了计算高效的可解释性分析，解决了推理质量与答案正确性分离的挑战

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have recently demonstrated significant improvements in complex reasoning. While quantifying generation uncertainty in LRMs is crucial, traditional methods are often insufficient because they do not provide finite-sample guarantees for reasoning-answer generation. Conformal prediction (CP) stands out as a distribution-free and model-agnostic methodology that constructs statistically rigorous uncertainty sets. However, existing CP methods ignore the logical connection between the reasoning trace and the final answer. Additionally, prior studies fail to interpret the origins of uncertainty coverage for LRMs as they typically overlook the specific training factors driving valid reasoning. Notably, it is challenging to disentangle reasoning quality from answer correctness when quantifying uncertainty, while simultaneously establishing theoretical guarantees for computationally efficient explanation methods. To address these challenges, we first propose a novel methodology that quantifies uncertainty in the reasoning-answer structure with statistical guarantees. Subsequently, we develop a unified example-to-step explanation framework using Shapley values that identifies a provably sufficient subset of training examples and their key reasoning steps to preserve the guarantees. We also provide theoretical analyses of our proposed methods. Extensive experiments on challenging reasoning datasets verify the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [131] [Weight Patching: Toward Source-Level Mechanistic Localization in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13694)
*Chenghao Sun,Chengsheng Zhang,Guanzheng Qin,Rui Dai,Xinmei Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Weight Patching方法，通过参数空间干预在相同架构的模型之间进行源导向分析，揭示模型能力表达的层级结构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机制可解释性方法主要关注激活空间定位和因果追踪，但激活空间中的重要模块可能只是聚合或放大上游信号，而非在自身参数中编码目标能力。需要参数空间的干预方法来填补这一空白。

Method: 提出Weight Patching方法：在具有相同架构但目标能力表达强度不同的配对模型（基础模型和行为专业化模型）之间，将专业化模型的选定模块权重替换到基础模型中。在指令跟随任务上实例化该方法，并引入基于向量锚点的行为接口框架，为开放式生成中任务相关控制状态的形成或恢复提供共享内部标准。

Result: 分析揭示了从浅层候选源侧载体到聚合路由模块，再到下游执行电路的层级结构。恢复的组件分数可以指导机制感知的模型融合，在评估的专家组合中改善选择性融合，并提供额外的外部验证。

Conclusion: Weight Patching填补了参数空间干预方法的空白，能够更精确地定位模型行为到内部组件，揭示能力表达的层级结构，并为模型融合提供机制感知的指导。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks to localize model behavior to the internal components that causally realize it. Prior work has advanced activation-space localization and causal tracing, but modules that appear important in activation space may merely aggregate or amplify upstream signals rather than encode the target capability in their own parameters. To address this gap, we propose Weight Patching, a parameter-space intervention method for source-oriented analysis in paired same-architecture models that differ in how strongly they express a target capability under the inputs of interest. Given a base model and a behavior-specialized counterpart, Weight Patching replaces selected module weights from the specialized model into the base model under a fixed input. We instantiate the method on instruction following and introduce a framework centered on a vector-anchor behavioral interface that provides a shared internal criterion for whether a task-relevant control state has been formed or recovered in open-ended generation. Under this framework, the analysis reveals a hierarchy from shallow candidate source-side carriers to aggregation and routing modules, and further to downstream execution circuits. The recovered component scores can also guide mechanism-aware model merging, improving selective fusion across the evaluated expert combinations and providing additional external validation.

</details>


### [132] [Rethinking AI Hardware: A Three-Layer Cognitive Architecture for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13757)
*Li Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Tri-Spirit Architecture将AI智能分解为规划、推理、执行三层，分别映射到不同计算硬件，通过异步消息总线协调，显著降低延迟和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统（云端AI、设备端推理、边缘-云端流水线）将规划、推理和执行视为单一过程，导致不必要的延迟、能耗和行为连续性碎片化。需要一种能充分利用异构硬件优势的智能结构。

Method: 提出Tri-Spirit三层认知框架：规划层（Super Layer）、推理层（Agent Layer）、执行层（Reflex Layer），每层映射到不同计算基板，通过异步消息总线协调。系统包括参数化路由策略、习惯编译机制（将重复推理路径提升为零推理执行策略）、收敛内存模型和显式安全约束。

Result: 在2000个合成任务的可重复模拟中，与云端中心和仅边缘基线相比，Tri-Spirit将平均任务延迟降低75.6%，能耗降低71.1%，LLM调用减少30%，实现77.6%的离线任务完成率。

Conclusion: 认知分解（而非仅模型缩放）是AI硬件系统级效率的主要驱动力。Tri-Spirit架构通过将智能分解到异构硬件，显著提升了自主AI系统的性能效率。

Abstract: The next generation of autonomous AI systems will be constrained not only by model capability, but by how intelligence is structured across heterogeneous hardware. Current paradigms -- cloud-centric AI, on-device inference, and edge-cloud pipelines -- treat planning, reasoning, and execution as a monolithic process, leading to unnecessary latency, energy consumption, and fragmented behavioral continuity. We introduce the Tri-Spirit Architecture, a three-layer cognitive framework that decomposes intelligence into planning (Super Layer), reasoning (Agent Layer), and execution (Reflex Layer), each mapped to distinct compute substrates and coordinated via an asynchronous message bus. We formalize the system with a parameterized routing policy, a habit-compilation mechanism that promotes repeated reasoning paths into zero-inference execution policies, a convergent memory model, and explicit safety constraints. We evaluate the architecture in a reproducible simulation of 2000 synthetic tasks against cloud-centric and edge-only baselines. Tri-Spirit reduces mean task latency by 75.6 percent and energy consumption by 71.1 percent, while decreasing LLM invocations by 30 percent and enabling 77.6 percent offline task completion. These results suggest that cognitive decomposition, rather than model scaling alone, is a primary driver of system-level efficiency in AI hardware.

</details>


### [133] [AlphaCNOT: Learning CNOT Minimization with Model-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13812)
*Jacopo Cossio,Daniele Lizzio Bosco,Riccardo Romanello,Giuseppe Serra,Carla Piazza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AlphaCNOT：基于蒙特卡洛树搜索的强化学习框架，用于CNOT门最小化，在线性可逆合成中比PMH基准减少32%门数，在拓扑约束版本中相比现有RL方案也有显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路优化对当前NISQ设备至关重要，因为错误传播通常随操作数量增加。CNOT门作为Clifford+T通用集中的唯一双量子比特门，其最小化问题尤为重要。现有启发式算法（如PMH）和基于强化学习的方法在拓扑约束合成中仍有改进空间。

Method: 提出AlphaCNOT，一种基于蒙特卡洛树搜索的强化学习框架，将CNOT最小化问题建模为规划问题。该方法为基于模型的方法，能够利用前瞻搜索评估未来轨迹，从而找到更高效的CNOT序列。

Result: 在线性可逆合成中，相比PMH基准实现了高达32%的CNOT门数减少；在拓扑约束版本中，在多种最多8量子比特的拓扑结构上，相比最先进的基于RL的解决方案实现了持续的门数减少。

Conclusion: 强化学习与基于搜索策略的结合可应用于不同的电路优化任务（如Clifford最小化），从而促进向"量子效用"时代的过渡。

Abstract: Quantum circuit optimization is a central task in Quantum Computing, as current Noisy Intermediate Scale Quantum devices suffer from error propagation that often scales with the number of operations. Among quantum operations, the CNOT gate is of fundamental importance, being the only 2-qubit gate in the universal Clifford+T set. The problem of CNOT gates minimization has been addressed by heuristic algorithms such as the well-known Patel-Markov-Hayes (PMH) for linear reversible synthesis (i.e., CNOT minimization with no topological constraints), and more recently by Reinforcement Learning (RL) based strategies in the more complex case of topology-aware synthesis, where each CNOT can act on a subset of all qubits pairs. In this work we introduce AlphaCNOT, a RL framework based on Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) that address effectively the CNOT minimization problem by modeling it as a planning problem. In contrast to other RL- based solution, our method is model-based, i.e. it can leverage lookahead search to evaluate future trajectories, thus finding more efficient sequences of CNOTs. Our method achieves a reduction of up to 32% in CNOT gate count compared to PMH baseline on linear reversible synthesis, while in the constraint version we report a consistent gate count reduction on a variety of topologies with up to 8 qubits, with respect to state-of-the-art RL-based solutions. Our results suggest the combination of RL with search-based strategies can be applied to different circuit optimization tasks, such as Clifford minimization, thus fostering the transition toward the "quantum utility" era.

</details>


### [134] [AI-Assisted Peer Review at Scale: The AAAI-26 AI Review Pilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13940)
*Joydeep Biswas,Sheila Schoepp,Gautham Vasan,Anthony Opipari,Arthur Zhang,Zichao Hu,Sebastian Joseph,Matthew Lease,Junyi Jessy Li,Peter Stone,Kiri L. Wagstaff,Matthew E. Taylor,Odest Chadwicke Jenkins*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AAAI-26首次大规模部署AI辅助同行评审，为22,977篇论文生成AI评审，调查显示参与者认为AI评审在技术准确性和研究建议方面优于人工评审


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学同行评审面临投稿量激增的压力，难以维持评审质量、一致性和及时性。虽然AI在评审中的应用被考虑，但关键未解问题是AI能否在现实会议规模下生成技术可靠的评审

Method: 结合前沿模型、工具使用和安全保障的多阶段流程，在不到一天内为所有22,977篇完整评审论文生成AI评审。系统在AAAI-26主轨道所有投稿中部署，每个投稿收到一个明确标识的AI评审

Result: 大规模调查显示，AAAI-26作者和程序委员会成员不仅认为AI评审有用，而且在技术准确性、研究建议等关键维度上更偏好AI评审而非人工评审。新基准测试表明系统在检测科学弱点方面显著优于简单的LLM生成评审基线

Conclusion: 最先进的AI方法已能在会议规模上对科学同行评审做出有意义的贡献，为下一代人机协同评估研究开辟了道路

Abstract: Scientific peer review faces mounting strain as submission volumes surge, making it increasingly difficult to sustain review quality, consistency, and timeliness. Recent advances in AI have led the community to consider its use in peer review, yet a key unresolved question is whether AI can generate technically sound reviews at real-world conference scale. Here we report the first large-scale field deployment of AI-assisted peer review: every main-track submission at AAAI-26 received one clearly identified AI review from a state-of-the-art system. The system combined frontier models, tool use, and safeguards in a multi-stage process to generate reviews for all 22,977 full-review papers in less than a day. A large-scale survey of AAAI-26 authors and program committee members showed that participants not only found AI reviews useful, but actually preferred them to human reviews on key dimensions such as technical accuracy and research suggestions. We also introduce a novel benchmark and find that our system substantially outperforms a simple LLM-generated review baseline at detecting a variety of scientific weaknesses. Together, these results show that state-of-the-art AI methods can already make meaningful contributions to scientific peer review at conference scale, opening a path toward the next generation of synergistic human-AI teaming for evaluating research.

</details>


### [135] [Reward Design for Physical Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13993)
*Derek Lilienthal,Manisha Mukherjee,Sameera Horawalavithana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析了GRPO训练中不同奖励信号对VLM物理推理行为的影响，发现奖励设计诱导领域特定的推理行为而非统一提升性能，其中基于注意力的内部奖励无需空间标注即可显著提升空间关系推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管SFT和GRPO等后训练算法在语言模型中显示出强大的推理增益，但奖励设计如何影响VLM的物理推理行为仍不清楚。当前最先进的VLM在物理基准测试上远低于人类表现，需要理解不同奖励信号对物理推理行为的具体影响。

Method: 采用系统奖励消融研究，比较四种语义丰富度递增的奖励信号：格式合规性、答案准确性、复合评分奖励（答案正确性、物理原理识别和单位一致性）、以及基于模型对输入图像区域注意力权重的新型内部奖励。在PhyX基准（3000个问题，涵盖6个物理领域和6种推理类型）上使用IBM Granite Vision 3.3 (2B)模型进行评估。

Result: 基于准确性的奖励在大多数领域优于SFT，但增益因奖励类型和领域而异。奖励设计不统一提升性能，而是诱导领域特定的推理行为：准确性奖励提供最强整体增益；评分奖励提高结构化推理质量但无一致准确性改进；基于注意力的奖励增强空间推理但在符号领域降低性能；内部注意力权重奖励无需空间标注即可将空间关系准确性从0.27提升至0.50。

Conclusion: 奖励设计对VLM物理推理行为具有领域特异性影响，监督模型在生成过程中的注意力分布是视觉基础物理推理的有前景方向。基于注意力的内部奖励展示了无需空间标注即可显著提升空间推理能力的潜力。

Abstract: Physical reasoning over visual inputs demands tight integration of visual perception, domain knowledge, and multi-step symbolic inference. Yet even state-of-the-art Vision Language Models (VLMs) fall far short of human performance on physics benchmarks. While post-training algorithms such as Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) have demonstrated strong reasoning gains in language models, how reward design shapes VLM physical reasoning behavior remains poorly understood. We present a systematic reward ablation study for GRPO-based VLM training on physical reasoning. We compare four reward signals of increasing semantic richness: format compliance, answer accuracy, a composite rubric reward (answer correctness, physics principle identification, and unit consistency), and a novel internal reward derived from model attention weights over input image regions. We evaluate on PhyX, a 3,000-problem benchmark spanning six physics domains and six reasoning types across multiple-choice and open-ended formats, using IBM Granite Vision 3.3 (2B). Across both formats, GRPO with accuracy-based rewards outperforms SFT on most domains, though gains vary substantially by reward type and domain. Reward design does not uniformly improve performance. Instead, it induces domain-specific reasoning behaviors. Accuracy-based rewards provide the strongest overall gains. Rubric rewards improve structured reasoning quality without consistent accuracy improvements. Attention-based rewards enhance spatial reasoning while degrading performance in symbolic domains. Our internal attention-weight reward requires no spatial annotations and improves spatial relation accuracy from 0.27 to 0.50, suggesting that supervising where the model attends during generation is a promising direction for visually grounded physical reasoning.

</details>


### [136] [Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning with Runtime Safety Shielding for Power Grid Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14032)
*Gitesh Malik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个安全约束的层次控制框架，将强化学习的高层决策与实时安全防护分离，通过运行时安全屏蔽确保电网操作的安全性，实现零样本泛化到未见电网拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在电网操作中虽有潜力，但在实际部署中面临严格安全要求、罕见扰动下的脆弱性以及对未见电网拓扑泛化能力差等限制。安全关键基础设施不能容忍灾难性故障，学习型控制器必须在硬物理约束内运行。

Method: 提出安全约束的层次控制框架：高层强化学习策略提出抽象控制动作，确定性运行时安全屏蔽通过快速前向模拟过滤不安全动作。安全作为运行时不变约束强制执行，独立于策略质量或训练分布。

Result: 在Grid2Op基准套件上评估，包括正常条件、强制线路中断压力测试和在ICAPS 2021大规模输电网上零样本部署。结果显示：平坦强化学习策略在压力下脆弱，纯安全方法过于保守；而提出的层次化安全感知方法实现更长的episode生存时间、更低的峰值线路负载，以及对未见电网的鲁棒零样本泛化。

Conclusion: 电网控制中的安全性和泛化能力最好通过架构设计而非复杂的奖励工程实现，为现实世界能源系统中可部署的学习型控制器提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has shown promise for automating power-grid operation tasks such as topology control and congestion management. However, its deployment in real-world power systems remains limited by strict safety requirements, brittleness under rare disturbances, and poor generalization to unseen grid topologies. In safety-critical infrastructure, catastrophic failures cannot be tolerated, and learning-based controllers must operate within hard physical constraints.
  This paper proposes a safety-constrained hierarchical control framework for power-grid operation that explicitly decouples long-horizon decision-making from real-time feasibility enforcement. A high-level reinforcement learning policy proposes abstract control actions, while a deterministic runtime safety shield filters unsafe actions using fast forward simulation. Safety is enforced as a runtime invariant, independent of policy quality or training distribution.
  The proposed framework is evaluated on the Grid2Op benchmark suite under nominal conditions, forced line-outage stress tests, and zero-shot deployment on the ICAPS 2021 large-scale transmission grid without retraining. Results show that flat reinforcement learning policies are brittle under stress, while safety-only methods are overly conservative. In contrast, the proposed hierarchical and safety-aware approach achieves longer episode survival, lower peak line loading, and robust zero-shot generalization to unseen grids.
  These results indicate that safety and generalization in power-grid control are best achieved through architectural design rather than increasingly complex reward engineering, providing a practical path toward deployable learning-based controllers for real-world energy systems.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [A Momentum-based Stochastic Algorithm for Linearly Constrained Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13272)
*Chenyang Qiu,Mihitha Maithripala,Zongli Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于动量的增广拉格朗日方法，用于线性约束非凸随机优化，使用Polyak型梯度估计器，每次迭代仅需一次随机梯度评估，达到O(ε⁻⁴)的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决线性约束非凸随机优化问题，其中目标函数光滑但只能获得有界方差的随机梯度。现有方法通常需要多次梯度评估或收敛速度较慢，需要设计更高效的算法。

Method: 提出动量增强的增广拉格朗日方法，采用Polyak型梯度估计器（递归动量），每次迭代仅需一次随机梯度评估。算法结合了动量技术和增广拉格朗日框架来处理线性约束。

Result: 在标准随机oracle模型和期望目标光滑性条件下，建立了原始约束问题一阶KKT残差的收敛保证。算法在期望意义下计算ε-稳定解需要O(ε⁻⁴)次随机梯度评估。

Conclusion: 该方法在迭代复杂度和实际计算效率上均优于代表性递归动量基线，为线性约束非凸随机优化提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper studies a stochastic algorithm for linearly constrained nonconvex optimization, where the objective function is smooth but only unbiased stochastic gradients with bounded variance are available. We propose a momentum-based augmented Lagrangian method that employs a Polyak-type gradient estimator and requires only one stochastic gradient evaluation per iteration. Under the standard stochastic oracle model and the smoothness condition of the expected objective, we establish a convergence guarantee in terms of the first-order KKT residual of the original constrained problem. In particular, the proposed method computes an $ε$-stationary solution in expectation within $O(ε^{-4})$ stochastic gradient evaluations. Numerical experiments further show that the proposed method achieves competitive iteration complexity and improved wall-clock efficiency compared with representative recursive-momentum baselines.

</details>


### [138] [A simple approach to the Løkka-Zervos dichotomy for absolutely continuous dividend strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13302)
*Tommy Mastromonaco,Nacer Fendri,Jean-François Renaud,Clarence Simard*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文重新研究了带资本注入的布朗风险模型中的分红优化问题，采用绝对连续分红策略（带仿射界）和奇异资本注入，并引入破产成本参数。研究发现最优解呈现Løkka-Zervos二分法结构：要么通过救助支付避免破产，要么不注入资本允许破产；两种情况下当盈余超过阈值时都以全速支付分红。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视Løkka & Zervos (2008)在布朗风险模型中带资本注入可能性的分红最大化问题。采用Alvarez & Shepp (1998)、Renaud & Simard (2021)、Renaud et al. (2023)引入的方法，考虑绝对连续分红策略（带仿射界）和奇异资本注入，并引入破产成本参数，以更全面地分析最优分红策略。

Method: 采用绝对连续分红策略（支付率有仿射界）和奇异资本注入。在性能函数中引入破产成本参数（破产惩罚）。通过分析框架提供明确条件来表达Løkka-Zervos二分法，使用资本注入成本或破产成本作为标准。进行数值分析以展示在AC仿射界结构下产生的参数值范围。

Result: 最优解呈现Løkka-Zervos二分法：要么通过救助支付避免破产，要么不注入资本允许破产；两种情况下当盈余超过阈值时都以全速支付分红。提供了表达该二分法的明确条件，揭示了解决方案的底层结构。对于某些参数值，最优策略是清算。数值分析展示了在AC仿射界结构下产生的参数值范围。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了带资本注入的分红优化问题分析框架，通过引入绝对连续分红策略和破产成本参数，揭示了最优策略的Løkka-Zervos二分法结构。研究提供了明确的判别条件，有助于理解资本注入成本与破产成本之间的权衡，并为实际风险管理决策提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We revisit the optimization problem solved in Løkka & Zervos (2008), i.e., the maximization of dividends, in a Brownian risk model, with the possibility (not the obligation) of making capital injections. Following the approach introduced in Alvarez & Shepp (1998), Renaud & Simard (2021), Renaud et al. (2023), we consider instead absolutely continuous (AC) dividend strategies with an affine bound on the payment rates, while singular capital injections are still allowed. In addition, we incorporate a parameter for the cost of ruin or, said differently, a penalty at ruin in the performance function. We show that the solution is a so-called Løkka-Zervos dichotomy: the surplus is never ruined by making bail-out payments, or no capital is injected and bankruptcy can occur; in either case, dividends are paid at full rate when the surplus is above a threshold. Our framework allows us to provide explicit conditions to express the dichotomy, either using the cost of capital injections or the cost of ruin as a criterion, which also exposes the underlying structure of the solution. In particular, for some values of the parameters, we show that it is optimal to liquidate. Moreover, we perform a numerical analysis highlighting the range of values generated under this AC affine-bound structure.

</details>


### [139] [Revisiting the Constant Stepsize Stochastic Approximation with Decision-Dependent Markovian Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13378)
*Hadi Hadavi,Wenlong Mou,Sergey Samsonov,Hoi-To Wai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文重新分析带决策依赖马尔可夫噪声的常步长随机逼近算法，重点刻画其稳态偏差相对于均值场方程根的特性。建立了决策依赖场景下的有限时间p阶矩界，证明了稳态偏差为O(α)阶，并建立了几何弱收敛性和函数中心极限定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带决策依赖马尔可夫噪声的常步长随机逼近算法的收敛性，特别是稳态偏差的量化分析。现有分析通常假设噪声为独立同分布或状态依赖，而决策依赖场景更具挑战性，需要新的分析框架。

Method: 首先建立决策依赖场景下随机逼近迭代的有限时间p阶矩界作为稳定性基础。然后利用泊松方程解的局部正则性条件（称为Poisson-Gateaux可微性WD*），分析稳态偏差。通过几何遍历性理论建立联合过程的几何弱收敛，并推导函数中心极限定理。

Result: 证明了在广泛的决策依赖场景下，稳态偏差为步长α的O(α)阶。建立了随机逼近迭代的几何弱收敛性，收敛到唯一的平稳分布。推导了函数中心极限定理，描述了算法在稳态附近的渐近正态性。

Conclusion: 本文为带决策依赖马尔可夫噪声的常步长随机逼近提供了完整的收敛性分析框架。松弛的正则性条件使得分析能够覆盖非光滑核的情况，如接受-拒绝机制、投影Langevin动力学和截断状态动力学等实际应用场景。

Abstract: We revisit the convergence analysis of constant stepsize stochastic approximation (SA) with decision-dependent Markovian noise, with a focus on characterizing the stationary bias against the root of the mean-field equation. We first establish the finite-time $p$-th moment bounds for the SA iterates in a general decision-dependent setting, which serve as a stability foundation for the subsequent analysis. Building on this foundation, and leveraging a local regularity condition termed Poisson--Gateaux differentiability (WD$^\ast$) for the solution to Poisson equation induced by the decision-dependent Markov kernel, we show that the stationary bias is of the order $\mathcal{O}(α)$ for a broad class of decision-dependent settings. Additionally, we establish geometric weak convergence of the joint SA process towards a unique stationary distribution, and a functional central limit theorem. Our relaxed regularity condition enables us to cover cases of non-smooth kernels such as acceptance--rejection mechanisms, projected Langevin dynamics, and clipped state dynamics.

</details>


### [140] [Convergence of the Iterates of the Stochastic Proximal Gradient Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13388)
*Javier I. Madariaga*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出对随机近端梯度方法的新研究，用于最小化两个凸函数之和，其中一个光滑。在适当假设下，无需随机变量方差的有界性或控制，证明了迭代几乎必然收敛和均值收敛到最小化问题的解。结果应用于分类和凸可行性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机近端梯度方法在优化两个凸函数之和时的收敛性，其中一个函数光滑。传统方法通常需要随机变量方差的有界性或控制条件，本文旨在放宽这些要求，建立更一般的收敛理论。

Method: 采用随机近端梯度方法，该方法结合了随机梯度下降和近端算子的思想。在适当的假设条件下（不要求随机变量方差有界或可控制），通过理论分析证明了算法的收敛性质。

Result: 证明了迭代序列几乎必然收敛到最小化问题的解，同时也在均值意义下收敛。这些收敛结果在不需要传统方差控制条件下成立，扩展了随机近端梯度方法的理论适用范围。

Conclusion: 本文建立了随机近端梯度方法在更宽松条件下的收敛理论，无需随机变量方差的有界性或控制。理论结果在分类和凸可行性问题中得到应用验证，为相关优化问题提供了更通用的算法保证。

Abstract: We propose a novel study of the stochastic proximal gradient method for minimizing the sum of two convex functions, one of which is smooth. Under suitable assumptions and without requiring any boundedness or control of the variance of the random variables, we derive the almost sure convergence and the convergence in the mean of the iterates to a solution of the minimization problem. The results are applied to classification and convex feasibility problems.

</details>


### [141] [A short proof of near-linear convergence of adaptive gradient descent under fourth-order growth and convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13393)
*Damek Davis,Dmitriy Drusvyatskiy*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Lyapunov函数的简化分析框架，用于证明梯度下降在凸函数且具有唯一极小值点时的局部收敛性，相比原论文的复杂分析更直接，并得到了更自适应的算法变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原论文（Davis, Drusvyatskiy, Jiang）证明了梯度下降在远离极小值点至少四次增长的平滑函数上具有局部近线性收敛性，但分析过程复杂，依赖于监测算法相对于"沟壑"（ravine）流形的性能。本文旨在提供更简洁的分析方法。

Method: 采用基于Lyapunov函数的直接论证方法，绕过原论文的复杂分析。该方法适用于目标函数为凸函数且具有唯一极小值点的情形。作为分析的副产品，得到了比原算法更具自适应性的变体。

Result: 成功建立了更简洁的收敛性证明框架，并开发了更自适应的算法变体。数值实验表明该变体具有令人鼓舞的性能表现。

Conclusion: 对于凸函数且具有唯一极小值点的情形，可以通过Lyapunov函数方法简化梯度下降局部收敛性的分析，同时得到性能更好的自适应算法变体。

Abstract: Davis, Drusvyatskiy, and Jiang showed that gradient descent with an adaptive stepsize converges locally at a nearly-linear rate for smooth functions that grow at least quartically away from their minimizers. The argument is intricate, relying on monitoring the performance of the algorithm relative to a certain manifold of slow growth -- called the ravine. In this work, we provide a direct Lyapunov-based argument that bypasses these difficulties when the objective is in addition convex and a has a unique minimizer. As a byproduct of the argument, we obtain a more adaptive variant than the original algorithm with encouraging numerical performance.

</details>


### [142] [Deepbullwhip: An Open-Source Simulation and Benchmarking for Multi-Echelon Bullwhip Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13478)
*Mansur M. Arief*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: deepbullwhip是一个开源Python包，提供模块化供应链仿真引擎和标准化基准测试框架，用于研究牛鞭效应，在半导体供应链实验中展示了427倍的累积放大效应和显著的计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛鞭效应在运营中持续存在，但缺乏模块化开源仿真工具用于多级库存动态（含非对称成本），以及缺乏标准化基准测试协议来比较不同缓解策略。

Method: 开发deepbullwhip开源Python包，包含：1）串行供应链仿真引擎（可插拔需求生成器、订购策略、成本函数，向量化蒙特卡洛引擎实现50-90倍加速）；2）基于注册表的基准测试框架（包含订购策略、预测方法、6种牛鞭指标、需求数据集如WSTS半导体账单数据）。

Result: 在四级半导体供应链实验中：1）累积放大427倍（1000条路径的蒙特卡洛均值）；2）上游层级存在随机过滤现象（CV=0.01）；3）超指数交货时间敏感性；4）2080万个仿真单元在7秒内完成。基准测试显示：Order-Up-To策略下，合成AR(1)与真实WSTS数据的牛鞭严重程度相差155倍；量化了BWR-NSAmp权衡，表明单一指标无法捕捉策略质量。

Conclusion: deepbullwhip填补了牛鞭效应研究的工具空白，提供了标准化仿真和基准测试能力。研究揭示了真实数据与合成数据的显著差异，以及牛鞭效应指标的局限性，为供应链管理提供了重要见解。

Abstract: The bullwhip effect remains operationally persistent despite decades of analytical research. Two computational deficiencies hinder progress: the absence of modular open-source simulation tools for multi-echelon inventory dynamics with asymmetric costs, and the lack of a standardized benchmarking protocol for comparing mitigation strategies across shared metrics and datasets. This paper introduces deepbullwhip, an open-source Python package that integrates a simulation engine for serial supply chains (with pluggable demand generators, ordering policies, and cost functions via abstract base classes, and a vectorized Monte Carlo engine achieving 50 to 90 times speedup) with a registry-based benchmarking framework shipping a curated catalog of ordering policies, forecasting methods, six bullwhip metrics, and demand datasets including WSTS semiconductor billings. Five sets of experiments on a four-echelon semiconductor chain demonstrate cumulative amplification of 427x (Monte Carlo mean across 1,000 paths), a stochastic filtering phenomenon at upstream tiers (CV = 0.01), super-exponential lead time sensitivity, and scalability to 20.8 million simulation cells in under 7 seconds. Benchmark experiments reveal a 155x disparity between synthetic AR(1) and real WSTS bullwhip severity under the Order-Up-To policy, and quantify the BWR-NSAmp tradeoff across ordering policies, demonstrating that no single metric captures policy quality.

</details>


### [143] [Semidefinite relaxations for nonlinear elasticity with energies convex in the Cauchy-Green strain tensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13566)
*Didier Henrion,Milan Korda,Martin Kružík,Karolına Sehnalová*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在非线性弹性中，对于满足特定条件的非凸能量函数，原始变分问题与其基于占据测度的线性矩表述之间不存在松弛间隙，从而保证了Lasserre矩-SOS层次结构的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非线性弹性中，寻找最小化给定存储能量密度的材料变形是一个具有挑战性的变分问题，可能不存在极小值：能量最优的材料会形成无限精细的微结构（皱纹）而不是平滑变形。需要开发有效的数值方法来避免传统有限元方法中的网格依赖性伪影。

Method: 使用Le Dret-Raoult半定投影公式计算能量函数的拟凸包络，证明原始非凸变分问题与其基于占据测度的线性矩表述之间不存在松弛间隙。利用Lasserre矩-平方和（SOS）层次结构，提供一种计算高效、无网格的数值方法。

Result: 证明了在能量函数满足框架不变性且关于Cauchy-Green应变张量凸的条件下，不存在松弛间隙，保证了Lasserre矩-SOS层次结构的收敛性。在边界条件线性且函数在应变张量中SOS凸的附加条件下，Lasserre层次结构的第一松弛是精确的。

Conclusion: 该研究为非线性弹性中的变分问题提供了一种新的计算框架，通过矩-SOS层次结构避免了有限元方法的网格依赖性伪影，并在特定条件下将拟凸包络的计算简化为小型凸半定优化问题。

Abstract: In nonlinear elasticity, finding the deformation of a material which minimizes a given stored energy density is a challenging calculus of variations problem which may fail to have minimizers: the energy optimal material forms infinitely fine microstructures (wrinkles) rather than deforming smoothly. In the case where the energy function is non-convex but frame indifferent and convex with respect to the Cauchy-Green strain tensor, we use the standard Le Dret-Raoult semidefinite projection formula for the quasiconvex envelope of the energy function to prove that there is no relaxation gap between the original non-convex calculus of variations problem and its linear moment formulation based on occupation measures. This implies convergence of the Lasserre moment-sum-of-squares (SOS) hierarchy and provides a computationally efficient, mesh-free numerical method that, unlike the finite element method, avoids undesirable mesh-dependent artifacts. Under the additional condition that the boundary condition is linear and the function is SOS convex in the strain tensor, we show that the first relaxation of the Lasserre hierarchy is exact. In other words, computing the quasiconvex envelope at a point boils down to solving a small convex semidefinite optimization problem.

</details>


### [144] [Observability from measurable sets for strongly coupled parabolic systems via single-component observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13599)
*Xiaoyu Fu,Gengsheng Wang,Huaiqiang Yu,Xiaomin Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文为强耦合抛物系统建立了从时空可测集的能观性不等式，其中观测仅作用于单个分量，克服了强耦合导致的高频振荡抵消问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究强耦合抛物系统的能观性问题，这类系统由复系数抛物方程驱动，是强耦合系统的典型代表。主要挑战在于强耦合会导致观测分量出现高频振荡抵消，使得逐点时间插值能观性估计失效。

Method: 开发了基于Remez型不等式的积分型插值能观性不等式，结合Phung-Wang和Apraiz等人从可测集推导能观性的策略，建立了从时空可测集的能观性不等式。

Result: 成功建立了从时空可测集对强耦合抛物系统的能观性不等式，克服了强耦合导致的高频振荡问题，实现了仅通过单个分量的观测就能控制整个系统。

Conclusion: 通过新的积分型插值能观性不等式和现有策略的结合，成功解决了强耦合抛物系统从可测集的能观性问题，为这类系统的控制理论提供了重要工具。

Abstract: We establish an observability inequality from space-time measurable sets for a class of strongly coupled parabolic systems consisting of two equations, where the observation acts on a single-component. The model is motivated by parabolic equations with complex coefficients and serves as a prototypical example of strongly coupled systems. The main difficulty lies in the fact that, unlike in the scalar and weakly coupled cases, pointwise-in-time interpolation observability estimates fail, as the observed component may exhibit high-frequency oscillatory cancellations induced by the coupling. To overcome this difficulty, we develop a new integral-type interpolation observability inequality based on a Remez-type inequality. With the aid of this integral-type interpolation observability inequality and the strategy developed in [Phung and Wang, JEMS, (2013), 681--703] and [Apraiz, Escauriaza,Wang and Zhang, JEMS, (2014), 2433--2475] for deriving observability from measurable sets, we obtain the desired observability inequality.

</details>


### [145] [Sum-of-Squares Stability Verification on Manifolds with Applications in Spacecraft Attitude Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13727)
*Fabian Geyer,Friedrich Tuttas,Walter Fichter,Torbjørn Cunis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一个框架，使用LaSalle不变原理和平方和规划验证航天器姿态控制系统的几乎全局渐近稳定性，简化Lyapunov函数搜索


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天器姿态控制中常使用方向向量或四元数等参数化方法避免姿态表示的奇异性，但这些参数带有单位约束，导致系统动力学在不可缩流形上演化，使稳定性分析复杂化

Method: 提出一个框架，结合LaSalle不变原理和平方和规划（SOS）编程，简化Lyapunov函数的搜索过程，用于验证此类系统的几乎全局渐近稳定性

Result: 将该框架应用于两个实例：1）在极低地球轨道利用空气动力学的两轴姿态捕获；2）在圆轨道受重力梯度力矩影响的三轴姿态捕获

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效验证航天器姿态控制系统的几乎全局渐近稳定性，为在非可缩流形上演化的动力学系统提供了一种系统化的稳定性分析方法

Abstract: In the context of spacecraft attitude control, parametrizations such as direction vectors or quaternions are often used to avoid singularities in the attitude representation. This, however, complicates the stability analysis of the system since, given the additional unit constraints, the resulting dynamics evolve on non-contractible manifolds. In this paper, we present a framework to verify almost global asymptotic stability of such systems using LaSalle's invariance principle and sum-of-squares programming, simplifying the search for Lyapunov functions. The framework is then applied to two examples: two-axis attitude acquisition utilizing aerodynamics in very low Earth orbits, and three-axis attitude acquisition for a satellite subject to gravity gradient torques in a circular orbit.

</details>


### [146] [Gradient Descent's Last Iterate is Often (slightly) Suboptimal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13870)
*Guy Kornowski,Ohad Shamir*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 证明了在凸Lipschitz函数优化中，对于GD和SGD的最后迭代点收敛，如果不预先知道总迭代次数T，任何步长序列都无法达到最优的1/√T收敛率，必须承受额外的对数因子损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究梯度下降(GD)和随机梯度下降(SGD)在凸Lipschitz函数优化中的最后迭代点收敛问题。Jain等人(2019)虽然通过构造非标准步长序列达到了最优的1/√T收敛率，但该序列需要预先知道总迭代次数T。他们推测如果不预先知道T，任何步长序列都无法确保SGD最后迭代点的最优误差，但这一推测一直未被证明。

Method: 通过理论证明的方法，证明了Jain等人的推测。研究不仅针对SGD，还扩展到无噪声的GD情况。证明表明，当考虑任意时间(last iterate guarantee)保证时，无法避免在T中出现额外的多对数因子。

Result: 证明了该推测的正确性：如果不预先知道总迭代次数T，任何步长序列都无法确保GD或SGD最后迭代点达到最优的1/√T收敛率。即使在无噪声的GD情况下，也必须承受Ω(log T/√T)的收敛率，即存在不可避免的多对数因子损失。

Conclusion: 对于凸Lipschitz函数优化，如果不预先知道总迭代次数T，任何步长序列都无法使GD或SGD的最后迭代点达到最优的1/√T收敛率，必须接受额外的对数因子损失。这种（略微）次优的停止时间在实践中是不可避免的普遍现象。

Abstract: We consider the well-studied setting of minimizing a convex Lipschitz function using either gradient descent (GD) or its stochastic variant (SGD), and examine the last iterate convergence. By now, it is known that standard stepsize choices lead to a last iterate convergence rate of $\log T/\sqrt{T}$ after $T$ steps. A breakthrough result of Jain et al. [2019] recovered the optimal $1/\sqrt{T}$ rate by constructing a non-standard stepsize sequence. However, this sequence requires choosing $T$ in advance, as opposed to common stepsize schedules which apply for any time horizon. Moreover, Jain et al. conjectured that without prior knowledge of $T$, no stepsize sequence can ensure the optimal error for SGD's last iterate, a claim which so far remained unproven. We prove this conjecture, and in fact show that even in the noiseless case of GD, it is impossible to avoid an excess poly-log factor in $T$ when considering an anytime last iterate guarantee. Our proof further suggests that such (slightly) suboptimal stopping times are unavoidably common.

</details>


### [147] [Anchored Spectral Estimator for Rigid Motion Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13915)
*Ziyue Zhao,Huikang Liu,Man-Chung Yue*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于谱方法的刚性运动同步新方法（ASE），在理论和实验上均优于传统的两阶段方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性运动同步问题在信号处理、机器人和计算机视觉中有广泛应用，但现有方法（特别是两阶段方法）存在局限性，需要更有效的解决方案

Method: 提出锚定谱估计器（ASE），这是一种基于几何考虑的谱方法，通过矩阵表示刚性运动并利用谱技术进行同步估计

Result: 理论上建立了ASE估计器的均匀误差界；实验表明ASE优于传统的两阶段方法，在多点集配准问题上也优于现有最优方法

Conclusion: ASE是一种有效的刚性运动同步方法，在理论和实际性能上均优于现有方法，具有广泛的应用前景

Abstract: A rigid motion in $\mathbb{R}^d$ consists of a proper rotation and a translation, and it can be represented as a matrix in $\mathbb{R}^{(d+1)\times (d+1)}$. The problem of rigid motion synchronization aims to estimate a collection of rigid motions $G^*_1, \dots, G^*_n$ from noisy observations of their comparisons ${G^*_i}^{-1} G^*_j$. Such problems naturally arise in diverse applications across signal processing, robotics, and computer vision, and have thus attracted intense research attention in recent years. Motivated by geometric considerations, this paper develops a novel spectral approach for rigid motion synchronization, called the anchored spectral estimator (ASE). Theoretically, we establish uniform estimation error bounds for the estimators produced by ASE. Empirically, we show that ASE outperforms the widely used two-stage approach, which first estimates the rotations and then the translations. Further numerical experiments on the multiple point-set registration problem are presented to demonstrate the superiority of ASE over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [148] [Optimal control of the coagulation-fragmentation equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13937)
*Enrico Sartor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究凝血-破碎方程的最优控制问题，通过时间依赖的标量控制调节凝血速率，目标函数包含控制项的二次惩罚和最终尺寸分布的终端成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究凝血-破碎过程的控制问题，探索如何使用低维执行器（单一时间依赖控制）来有效重塑无限维的粒子尺寸分布，这在粒子工程和材料科学中具有重要应用价值。

Method: 在加权L^1框架下，采用直接法证明最优控制的存在性；建立成本泛函的Γ-收敛性；对有界凝血核证明动力学可微性，推导伴随方程和庞特里亚金型最小值原理；使用投影梯度算法与Armijo回溯法进行数值求解。

Result: 证明了控制到状态映射的弱-弱连续性、最优控制的存在性、成本泛函Γ-收敛的稳定性；对有界核建立了可微性理论和最小值原理；数值实现验证了单一低维执行器能有效重塑无限维粒子尺寸分布。

Conclusion: 该研究为凝血-破碎过程的控制问题建立了完整的数学框架，证明了理论可行性，并通过数值实验验证了使用单一时间依赖控制调节凝血速率来有效控制粒子尺寸分布的实用性。

Abstract: We formulate and analyse an optimal control problem for the coagulation-fragmentation equation, where a scalar, time-dependent control modulates the coagulation rate by multiplying the coagulation kernel. The objective functional consists of a quadratic penalisation of the control and a terminal cost depending on the final size distribution. In a weighted $L^1$ framework, we prove weak-to-weak continuity of the control-to-state map under perturbations of the coefficients and obtain existence of optimal controls by the direct method. We then establish $Γ$-convergence of the corresponding cost functionals, providing stability of optimal controls and justifying truncation of unbounded kernels in the optimisation setting. For bounded coagulation kernels we show differentiability of the dynamics, derive an adjoint equation, and obtain a Pontryagin-type minimum principle. Lipschitz continuity of the gradient with respect to the control yields, at the continuous level, convergence of a projected-gradient algorithm with Armijo backtracking. A proof-of-concept finite-volume implementation is then used in a numerical study targeting the number of particles within a prescribed size window, demonstrating that a single low-dimensional actuator can effectively reshape an infinite-dimensional particle-size distribution.

</details>


### [149] [Understanding the Variance Dichotomy in Continuous Simulation Optimization: A Minimax Lower Bound Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13965)
*Jianzhong Du,L. Jeff Hong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了连续仿真优化中的方差二分现象，揭示了在有限仿真预算下，低噪声随机CSO与确定性CSO具有相似复杂度，但随着预算增加会过渡到较慢收敛机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献显示确定性CSO与随机CSO存在显著对比，随机设置的收敛率似乎对噪声方差大小不敏感。但这种渐近观点不能完全解释有限仿真预算下CSO的行为，特别是在低噪声设置中。

Method: 开发了极小极大下界分析，证明复杂度由方差依赖项和方差独立项的最大值决定。

Result: 当仿真预算不大且噪声方差较低时，方差独立项占主导，意味着低噪声随机CSO与确定性CSO具有基本相同复杂度。随着预算增加，方差依赖项变得主导，随机CSO的收敛行为过渡到由噪声方差和仿真预算共同决定的较慢机制。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了连续仿真优化中有限预算分析的空白，揭示了方差二分现象，为理解随机CSO在不同噪声水平和预算规模下的性能提供了理论框架。

Abstract: This paper studies the variance dichotomy in continuous simulation optimization (CSO). Existing literature shows a sharp contrast between deterministic CSO and stochastic CSO, with convergence rates in stochastic settings appearing insensitive to the magnitude of the noise variance. However, this asymptotic view does not fully explain the behavior of CSO under finite simulation budgets, especially in low-noise settings. To address this gap, this work develops a minimax lower-bound analysis and shows that the complexity is decided by the maximum of a variance-dependent term and a variance-independent term. When the simulation budget is not very large and the noise variance is low, the variance-independent term dominates, implying that low-noise stochastic CSO has essentially the same complexity as deterministic CSO. As the budget increases, the variance-dependent term becomes dominant, and the convergence behavior of stochastic CSO transitions to a slower regime determined jointly by the noise variance and the simulation budget.

</details>


### [150] [Multistage Conditional Compositional Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14075)
*Buse Şen,Yifan Hu,Daniel Kuhn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出多阶段条件组合优化(MCCO)新范式，结合多阶段随机规划和条件随机优化，解决嵌套条件期望和非线性成本函数最小化问题，应用包括最优停止、LQR、分布鲁棒上下文赌博机等


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统嵌套采样方法面临维度灾难，场景复杂度随嵌套层数指数增长，需要开发更高效的多级蒙特卡洛方法

Method: 开发新的多级蒙特卡洛技术，使场景复杂度仅随精度要求多项式增长，而非指数增长

Result: 提出的多级蒙特卡洛方法显著降低了计算复杂度，场景复杂度从指数增长改进为多项式增长

Conclusion: MCCO为不确定性下的决策提供了新范式，新方法有效克服了传统方法的维度灾难问题

Abstract: We introduce Multistage Conditional Compositional Optimization (MCCO) as a new paradigm for decision-making under uncertainty that combines aspects of multistage stochastic programming and conditional stochastic optimization. MCCO minimizes a nest of conditional expectations and nonlinear cost functions. It has numerous applications and arises, for example, in optimal stopping, linear-quadratic regulator problems, distributionally robust contextual bandits, as well as in problems involving dynamic risk measures. The naïve nested sampling approach for MCCO suffers from the curse of dimensionality familiar from scenario tree-based multistage stochastic programming, that is, its scenario complexity grows exponentially with the number of nests. We develop new multilevel Monte Carlo techniques for MCCO whose scenario complexity grows only polynomially with the desired accuracy.

</details>
